# Optional CUDA DLSS consumer: integration in progress

`engine/dlss.py` consumes the optional `superluxcore_dlss` C ABI. It requires a
complete set of fresh, matching float32 guide planes, scene/frame IDs, actual
jitter and backward motion. It never manufactures missing guides or treats
progressive accumulated beauty as an independent frame. Native failures raise
and leave raw input intact. Native-resolution alpha remains exact. The caller
must execute it on a worker and explicitly close it (a weakref finalizer also
owns native teardown).

There is currently no UI selection, default policy change or live viewport
connection. Those require the renderer-owned guide and sample contract first.
`dev-tools/dlss_backend_test.py` contains local contract tests and opt-in actual
CUDA tests for 720p frames, separate histories, worker calls, native alpha/raw
preservation, payload-memory plateau, resize, upscaling, failure recovery and
budget rejection. Skipped GPU tests are not GPU passes.

See `SuperLuxCore/doc/engineering/2026-10-16-dlss-cuda.md` in the sibling renderer
repository for the pinned Blender 5.3 beta source audit, verified RTX probe,
remaining producer requirements and evidence/packaging boundaries.

`dev-tools/dlss_render_frame_test.py` now exercises an actual SuperLuxCore tiled
producer for a procedural opaque-matte scene, with explicit native frame jitter,
fresh scrambles and complete per-pixel 1-SPP coverage. A ten-degree orbit of static
geometry derives backward motion from current/previous unjittered projections.
The matte-only specular/roughness values are known closure values and are not
general material approximations. This test recreates tiled sessions; it does not
establish the live viewport handoff or responsiveness.

`dlss_sequence_test.py` feeds these captured renderer planes to CUDA RR, preserving
raw inputs and native alpha. It keeps a moving-frame history average out of the
matched-SPP quality baseline. `dlss_export_visuals.py` exports linear 32-bit EXR
and ACES 2.0 PNG through stock Blender and reopens the EXR to check preservation.
Those image export checks are not stock Blender live DLSS/add-on packaging tests.

Final candidate identity, bounded CPU/Metal guide differences, default-preservation
checks and RTX replay/visual receipts are in workspace
`dev-tools/reviews/2026-10-16/dlss/`. Earlier candidate results remain distinct.

The first orbit test was invalidated by direct image review: Scene.Parse with
only camera origin reset the target, while guide projection assumed its previous
value. The corrected producer supplies origin/target/up/FOV and checks every hit's
pixel reprojection before accepting a frame. The corrected 16-frame orbit, native
stationary sequence and actual 2x SR sequence passed RTX replay. Selected orbit
frames 0,4,8,12,15 and SR were directly reviewed in ACES 2.0; raw EXR reopen error
is zero. The still-noisy 144-SPP comparison and bounded matte-only findings are
recorded in the renderer document and `dlss-report.json`, not a complete support
or live viewport claim.

The later native stage adds `RenderSession.GetDenoisingSurfaceGuides` and
per-hit OpenPBR/GGX metal/glass estimates. This is an optional host CPU reference,
including when Metal renders the beauty. `DLSSFrame.from_surface_guides` accepts
only complete supported primary surfaces, explicit motion planes and exact
correspondence to the actual rendered position AOV. It copies/compresses diffuse
albedo at the DLSS boundary without modifying renderer AOVs or raw beauty.
Background, unsupported materials and paths needing continuation are rejected.

Actual 720p Metal silhouette disagreements showed why this correspondence gate
is necessary. Those captures are retained as ineligible diagnostics and were
not passed to RTX. This does not constitute renderer-owned Metal/CUDA guides.

The textured OpenPBR metal/coated-plastic CPU fixture passed 16 independent
720p frames and a final RTX replay with the current consumer. Seven latest
contract/backend tests passed. ACES 2.0 images and exact-reopened EXRs were
inspected; noise is reduced, but broad highlight blotches and a +1.30% green
mean difference versus the noisy 256-SPP reference remain recorded. There is
no live viewport selection, history/presentation integration, production default
promotion or completed optional wheel/ZIP support. The sibling renderer document
and workspace `phase2-report.json` describe the exact current scope and failures.


## GPU primary guides and coherent RT frame leases (phase 3, in progress)

The final macOS native candidate is
`4c5fbe6f71d4b75ee1a6668a7c373097f821c017781414d9c88d2c71c1c0bfb9`.
Its isolated API-validation wheel is retained in
`test-scenes/validation-2026-10-16/dlss/phase3/wheel` (wheel SHA
`e6df19358d66aead67729e5020c0ac3a464386ba813e6f768724887cd08151d5`).
It does not contain the optional CUDA DLSS backend and is not a released add-on.
The 181-file kernel digest is
`3dffe7cba185506d02d9666bf00b913c9f7e690aa8a0b8e65fe7cedea2c7ef58`;
exact sources and translator/worker hashes are in `phase3/candidate-identity.json`.

- `film.denoisingguides.enable` records features at the actual GPU BSDF/hit.
  Its separate optional 32-float task record leaves the default task/sample ABI
  alone. TILEPATHOCL AA1 and RTPATHOCL are the initial supported producers.
  At the previously failing 720p jitter, guide positions are now bit-exact to
  Metal's own POSITION AOV. Matte, rough OpenPBR and Metal2 checks also preserve
  default-off/on beauty and all tested AOVs bit-exactly.
- `rtpath.denoisingframes.enable` uses bounded tiles (default 256; test 128),
  one complete fresh sample per pixel and global R2 camera jitter. It retains
  every contribution in the ordinary accumulating raw film, separately from
  the fresh per-frame film. Full camera coverage replaces sparse/adaptive/
  foveated sample selection only in this explicit denoising-frame mode.
  Light-path lanes are added after camera lanes; LT/hybrid combinations still
  need validation and light-only mode is explicitly rejected.
- `RenderSession.GetDenoisingFrame(after_id, timeout_ms)` returns an immutable
  latest-frame lease: HDR RGBA, 32-float guides, sample counts, exact uploaded
  camera matrices, jitter, monotonic frame ID and scene epoch. Read-only NumPy
  views retain their native owner through edit, resize and Stop. The publisher
  keeps one slot, invalidates it at edits/resizes and does not queue old frames.
- Camera-only edits retain the scene epoch. Material/geometry/light changes and
  resize invalidate history. The new RT loop drains a complete bounded tile
  set, resets TileWork when NextTile returns false, and keeps the sync worker
  responsive after natural halt so WaitForDone/HasDone/Stop do not deadlock.
- The Python `RendererDLSS` consumer commits history only after a successful
  evaluation. Backward motion bridges the previous **delivered** camera, even
  if renderer frame IDs were skipped. Failure, resize, epoch change or explicit
  cut resets history. Moving reflective pixels, incomplete guides, unsupported
  camera lens/distortion/shutter-time paths and moving meshes are rejected
  until the producer supplies their missing motion/continuation data.

The final candidate passed native Metal 720p frame/edit/resize/stop tests,
eight orbit edits plus a material history change, and a stock Blender 5.2.1
host smoke with isolated CPU/Metal native APIs. Camera/position projection
error stayed below 0.0004 pixels. These are renderer API tests; they do not
measure live Blender draws or heavy-scene interaction latency.

The earlier `73204da...` candidate supplied six actual Metal frames to RTX
5060 Ti DLSS (skipped IDs, camera translation with max 13.09-pixel backward
motion, then 97x65 resize). All inputs stayed unchanged, alpha was exact,
outputs finite, and the owned GPU process exited. The replay used about
496 MB RSS / 438 MB driver-reported GPU memory at peak. It remains attributed
to that capture candidate; it is not relabelled as the final publisher fix.

Stock Blender exported/reopened 720p float EXRs exactly and ACES 2.0 PNGs.
Direct review found coherent orientation/silhouettes and camera displacement,
with visible broad splotches and oversmoothing on the walls/spheres. Against
a still-noisy 256-SPP CPU reference, this bounded four-frame matte diagnostic
had linear MSE 0.0004453, versus 0.0192135 for the same four raw frames averaged.
This is no speed/equal-error claim, fine-detail proof or default promotion.
Matte/OpenPBR CPU preservation is exact. Final stock Blender Metal2 CPU
beauty differs by up to 0.0002523, exceeding earlier observations. Three
repeats on the same host show old-binary self-repeat max 0.0001464 and
new-binary max 0.00003146. These diagnostics do not establish a cause or
convert the failed global strict preservation gate into a pass.

The full original goal remains active: reflection/transmission/volume/hair
and general object/camera motion, worker-side live presentation with faithful
color handling/fallback, final still/animation/SR, resident CUDA context/stream
interop, stock optional packages, device-loss teardown and heavy real-scene
orbit/resize quality/latency gates are not complete.

For Linux preparation, the rejected whole-source archive was never sent.
The server downloaded verified public commits itself. A separately approved
77,508-byte DLSS-only patch (33 files, prior unrelated WIP filtered out) applies
to the public base; the private GCC14/Python3.13/Conan cache changes no system
compiler/driver. Core-only metadata excludes three unused GUI packages and a
private lib-to-lib64 symlink corrects a published cache layout mismatch.
Inspect the actual `phase3/server-build-candidate` process/receipt before
resuming; do not start duplicate builds or count the cancelled public
scaffold as a benchmark. This Linux candidate intentionally excludes unrelated
local WIP and cannot be called a full checkout/package validation.

Evidence: workspace `dev-tools/reviews/2026-10-16/dlss/phase3/`,
`dlss-report.json`, `dlss-checkpoint.json`; previous `phase2-report.json` remains.


### Live consumer and Linux native validation follow-up

A separate `engine/dlss_stream.py` consumer owns backend creation/evaluation/close
on one background thread. It performs a zero-timeout native lease read under
the session owner's lock, evaluates outside that lock, keeps one completed
result and discards results from an older session or applied edit. Shutdown
signals cancellation without waiting on the caller. Six contract tests cover
backpressure/skipped IDs, edit priority, stale-result discard, backend ownership,
session replacement, unavailable runtime suppression, and release/recovery of
failed guide leases. Errors store type/message only, never an exception traceback
that could retain a full native frame. This is an HDR consumer component, not
yet a Blender display path; image-pipeline and main-thread GL integration remain.

The Linux native probe found an actual unresolved `LPECompileExpression` import
symbol after the first successful build. Film calls the LPE compiler in
slg-core, creating a static-archive back-reference. The Linux-only CMake link
uses `LINK_GROUP:RESCAN` for slg-core/slg-film in both luxcore targets. The small
task-only supplementary patch is recorded separately from the approved native
DLSS patch. Mac library/kernel fingerprints remain unchanged. The candidate is
a verified public base plus task-only patches, not the full local WIP checkout.

Resource supervision retains a 4-GiB own-tree budget and 1.5-GiB SSD reserve.
Only owned reproducible PCH caches use temporary task-specific RAM storage; the
full cache byte size is conservatively added to process-tree RSS. At each slice
end caches are compacted or discarded and RAM storage removed. The v7 build
slice reached its 600-second deadline with no remaining child, after a 3.00-GB
conservative RSS-plus-cache peak. v8 preparation failed before Ninja: its empty
exception message was incorrectly treated as success by that temporary harness.
`server-v8-preparation-failure.json` explicitly invalidates it and records removal
of the orphaned 233-MB RAM cache. The corrected v9 supervisor checks exception
presence and cleans all of its declared RAM-cache filenames. Inspect its actual
receipt; none of the interrupted/preparation runs is a build/GPU/performance pass.

Further CPU diagnosis used the exact preserved old/current whole wheels in
stock Blender 5.2.1, eight fresh Metal2 sessions for each of one and four CPU
threads. At one thread both self-repeats and all 64 old/new cross-pairs are
bit-exact. At four threads both binaries vary: old self-max 0.0001463816, new
self-max 0.0001847148 in this batch. This isolates a scheduling-sensitive
diagnostic condition; its root cause remains unproven. No renderer default,
thread count or tolerance was changed, and the original four-thread strict
preservation failure remains failed. Both temporary whole-wheel trees were
removed after testing (about 381 MB total); unique wheels and NPZ/log evidence
remain. Evidence is in `phase3/cpu-thread-diagnostic/` and `stream-contract.log`.


## Actual CUDA producer, server migration and consumer teardown follow-up

The previous RTX 5060 Ti server completed its v9 native build and imported
`ec0b9a64c862e32b9041bb21874297d0371d1e3a8e8ea2a2a0f6432a446c949e`.
This Linux candidate is the pinned public core plus the audited task-only guide /
RT-frame and Linux static-rescan patches; it is not the full local Mac WIP.
Actual CUDA guide-on/off beauty and AOV buffers were bit-exact in the tested
720p matte and small rough OpenPBR/Metal2 fixtures. Fifteen synchronous DLSS
frames covered static accumulation, camera translation, eight orbits, material
edit and odd resize. Ten asynchronous CUDA-to-NGX frames covered camera edits,
resize and worker-owned backend teardown. Raw input hashes and alpha were
preserved. This is a procedural opaque-matte integration gate, not live Blender
GL display, general motion/material support, final animation or a heavy scene.

The successful diagnostic compiler policy was private NVRTC 12.9.86, SASS,
split=1, Ofast=mid, PCH disabled, and a separate compiler cache namespace. It
passed the existing 4-GiB test-tree bound. These settings were changed together;
no isolated compiler speedup or default-policy promotion is established. Earlier
12.8 OOM/reserve/deadline runs remain failures/non-results. Compile time and
case wall time are not rendering speed comparisons. The CUDA cache currently
omits some compiler policy/version identity; continue using explicit separate
namespaces until that broader engine issue is addressed.

Four actual-CUDA 720p images were exported with stock Blender 5.2.1 to ACES 2.0
PNG and raw float EXR. All EXRs reopened with zero error. Direct review found
coherent orientation, wall colours and large sphere silhouettes, and visible
noise reduction in the four-frame DLSS image versus the single noisy raw frame.
Broad blotches, oversmoothing and coarse low-sample shading remain, especially
after reset/material edit. Samples differ, and no equal-quality speed or temporal
quality claim follows. See `phase3/native-visual-review.json`,
`phase3/native-visual/visuals.json` and `phase3/native-cuda-pinned/`.

The user replaced the remote endpoint with `213.196.166.17:63818`. Task-local
`remote.py` now targets it; the previous endpoint is retired. Initial inventory
confirmed RTX 5060 Ti 16 GiB, about 60 GiB cgroup RAM, about 32 GiB SSD free and no
active GPU process. The complete Vast operating guide was read before changes.
A new owned directory is built from the same public commits and task-only files;
no unrelated local WIP, user scene, compiled cache archive or default Blender
installation is transferred/replaced. Native build has an explicit 16-GiB bound
on this larger server; GPU validation keeps the existing 4-GiB test bound and the
SSD reserve is increased to 3 GiB. All build/test children are supervised/reaped.

The first new-server optional backend test failed with NGX FeatureNotSupported.
Read-only inspection found that the compute-only host injected libcuda but no
`libnvidia-ngx.so.1`. Matching 595.91.07 userspace libraries were extracted from
the official NVIDIA archive into the task directory. The installer was never
run, host libcuda/libnvidia-ml were excluded, and no system linker configuration,
service or kernel driver was changed. Temporary archive/extraction trees were
removed. With this private search path, all seven backend contract/GPU tests
passed, including native-resolution alpha/input preservation, resize, optional
SR and independent histories. Their native-renderer gates are tracked separately
from the previous server; old passes are never reassigned to the new build.

A deterministic regression then reproduced a consumer teardown stall: after
SDK return, `DLSSFrameWorker` blocked on the session edit lock merely to publish
a result. Shutdown could not finish while the edit owner retained that lock.
Publication now uses a nonblocking lock; a busy owner causes the stale result to
be discarded, and backend teardown can proceed. The SDK still runs outside the
session lock. The new pre-fix failure and seven passing post-fix contract tests
are in `phase3/server2/stream-stop-repro.log` and `stream-contract.log`. The real
CUDA stream harness now uses a test-only post-SDK barrier to verify teardown
while the session lock is held. That barrier verifies CPU ownership/teardown;
it does not claim cancellation of an executing NGX kernel or Blender draw latency.

## Phase 4: coherent image pipeline and Blender presentation boundary

The current native candidates are Mac `38c6116867f25cf825b70861ff3f16e37ce305d686f1fcec0379af48f7511ccc`
and new-server Linux `1c5f739d797ddd691dea7bc7e6648b472ab69cead3adbc4b0b6f25863add1797`.
These replace neither the identity nor the scope of the retained phase-3 results.

A valid LightMix regression disabled every radiance group: the ordinary film was
zero while the previous independent-frame producer still returned nonzero RGB
(maximum 2.8722295761). `ImagePipeline::Copy()` now preserves radiance scales.
An earlier attempted comparison used a latest independent frame and 33 accumulated
frames at periodic halt; that comparison is confounded and explicitly invalid.
The zero-group invariant is the evidence for the fix, not that earlier difference.

`rtpath.denoisingframes.imagepipeline.enable` is opt-in and defaults to false.
It captures a CPU-only film/pipeline context, matching AOVs, alpha, metadata and
LightMix revision for the independent frame. `DenoisingImagePipeline.process()`
accepts the native frame lease and external float32 HDR from DLSS, applies the
original plugin chain without merging or scaling radiance twice, and returns an
owned RGBA array. It does not read the live session or a GPU context. Input alpha
is temporarily applied and restored using RAII; old leases remain usable after
Stop and after settings changes. Plugin state persists on its processing worker
and resets on scene/pipeline/size changes. Unsupported output sizes are rejected:
this does not yet implement auxiliary-channel resampling for SR.

The presentation channel payload cap defaults to 256 MiB. It excludes plugin
private allocations, scene metadata/maps, Python arrays, output buffers, the
ordinary/fresh films and SDK allocations. The prototype currently copies film
channels per frame; resident interop, pooling and full heavy-scene memory/latency
optimization remain open. An allocation/budget/plugin failure preserves ordinary
rendering and produces an explicit reason for raw fallback.

A RenderSession image-pipeline/LightMix Parse invalidates the published frame,
advances the required revision and rejects a concurrently prepared older frame.
The stream postprocessor runs outside the session edit lock, publishes a tagged
RGB/RGBA display result, coalesces redraw notifications and discards stale edits.
New optional DLSS viewport settings/runtime paths are exposed without changing
the default denoiser, renderer or physically based transport settings. The main
thread only accepts matching session/sequence pixels and performs GPU upload.
Unsupported hosts/materials/runtime failures retain the ordinary render path.

Current Mac and Linux native gates passed four pipeline cases (720p linear +
gamma, Reinhard, MIST/depth, depth switch), zero-group LightMix, unchanged raw
inputs, external alpha/restoration, malformed-buffer rejection, processing after
Stop, budget fallback and hot-setting invalidation. The native pipeline and an
independent merge reference were bit-exact in all four tested cases. Actual
CUDA/NGX delivered ten matching asynchronous frames including five camera edits
and 97x65 resize; 17 processed frames passed an analytical single-application
linear scale check at zero error. The held-session-lock post-SDK teardown passed.
This verifies ownership after a completed SDK call, not cancellation of an
executing NGX kernel. Seven actual backend/contract tests also passed.

Fresh stock Blender 5.2.1 native-host CPU defaults, 720p Metal guide preservation,
Metal independent-frame orbit/material/resize and the bounded one-thread Metal2
old/new diagnostic passed. All 64 one-thread cross-pairs were exact. The original
four-thread preservation failure remains open; no tolerance or default thread
count was lowered. The latest Mac profile was removed after these gates; its
unique wheel (`06f0fd6c8d25edf4df6bd509f923315b6e1a56828bdf2b8379e96b024ce51001`)
and all render/log/JSON evidence remain. The later Linux-only static Python import
fix is not relabelled as part of that already-tested Mac wheel.

The new server's official Blender 5.3 beta is build `5d8151335b6b`, archive SHA
`5034da66e2e2c49296b37e58434bf8d7dda919d2859e61ca8a69f4290eab0306`.
Missing Ubuntu user-space dependencies are privately extracted; no system driver,
linker or default installation is changed. A private Xvfb/PRoot display uses
llvmpipe OpenGL for functional UI testing while CUDA/NGX use the RTX GPU. This is
not a hardware-display or workstation responsiveness benchmark. Display children
are terminated while their parent can reap them; failed startup probes and their
cleanup receipts remain separate. A failed initial development wheel activation
and a static-package import failure are recorded. The latter assumed an optional
`pysuperluxcore.libs` directory existed; the package now skips that optional scan
when absent, after its native import already checked linker dependencies. The
exact private development wheel is rebuilt/installed together. Stock standalone
ZIP installation is still a separate unpassed gate.

Actual UI startup exposed generic `KernelCacheFill` compiling an unused PATHOCL
program before the requested coherent RTPATHOCL session. Only the explicit
independent-frame realtime path now skips that generic filler and compiles its
actual scene on the worker. Ordinary CUDA and existing Metal policies are
preserved and regression tested. The earlier UI attempt was deliberately
cancelled/reaped; it supplies no cold-start speed ratio. Actual view_draw orbit /
resize validation is in progress; inspect the final phase-4 report before
claiming it passed.

Two actual 720p asynchronous CUDA/NGX captures after the explicit linear .5 test
pipeline were exported with stock Blender ACES 2.0 and raw EXR. Reopen error was
zero. Direct review found coherent orientation and large silhouettes, with broad
smoothing/blotchy shadows still visible. No matched high-SPP/equal-error reference
was captured, so no quality/speed promotion follows. General reflection/object/
background/shutter motion, transmission/volumes/hair, heavy real-scene draws,
final still/animation, SR integration, device-loss teardown and optional shipped
packages remain unfinished. Evidence: `phase4/` under the workspace DLSS review.


### Phase-4 final UI result and limits

The final stock Blender 5.3 beta run passed actual view_update/view_draw,
CUDA/NGX, native postprocessing, 11 DLSS uploads, four orbit edits, two real
area resizes, missing-runtime ordinary-image fallback and DLSS restoration.
The same native RenderSession was preserved during both runtime transitions.
`producer_available()` separates renderer eligibility from optional SDK paths:
on an explicitly selected eligible CUDA/DLSS session, coherent frame production
remains configured while the display worker falls back or recovers. Unsupported
hardware/backends still use ordinary rendering without coherent-frame requests.
This avoids changing physical rendering and starting a cold compile simply
because a DLL/runtime path changes. Existing defaults and transport settings are
unchanged; the procedural test explicitly uses opaque matte RGB.

Before this fix, the runtime switch changed renderer config, started recompiling
and the supervised run exited with SIGSEGV (-11) during shutdown after a teardown
deadline. Its failed receipt/log remain separate. The initial crash-text file was
removed with test temporaries before it was copied; that backtrace is unavailable.
The harness now preserves future crash-text logs before cleanup and refuses to
unload Blender underneath a worker that did not drain, leaving bounded termination
to its external supervisor. The particular fixed transition now exits 0 with all
workers/display children reaped. This is not proof of general device-loss/C-ABI
teardown safety; that broader gate remains open.

The first whole-window PNG was entirely black and is INVALID visual evidence.
The initial UI success therefore established event/result/upload ownership only.
Direct live-framebuffer reads subsequently proved visible, finite image content.
The naive one-pixel live-region comparison failed (.00275 in a retained diagnostic,
and up to .00695 in the final sequence); it remains unresolved and is not marked
passed. A distinct controlled orthographic draw of the same actual uploaded
RGB16F texture/batch passed the unchanged .002 threshold: maximum .0001381636.
The two checks have different scopes; neither erases the earlier failed oracle.

Direct live GPU captures for static DLSS, ordinary fallback and restored DLSS
were exported at 1311x878 with stock Blender ACES2 PNG and raw 32-bit EXR; EXR
reopen error was zero. Direct visual review confirmed white/blue sphere placement,
orientation and coherent silhouettes. Fallback is noisier and darker, while DLSS
is brighter and strongly smoothed. Correct radiance/brightness retention and fine
detail are not established without a matched reference. No quality promotion.

The successful functional UI run used 3,592,601,600 bytes sampled test-tree plus
supervisor RSS (4-GiB cap) and 2,231,369,728 bytes observed compute VRAM. The separate
software X display is outside that test-tree RSS figure. Process and display
cleanup passed. Orbit new-image delays were 3.83–4.93 s and resize delays 7.99 /
12.32 s in this software-GL environment. These delays remain poor; there is no
production responsiveness pass, hardware-display comparison or speed ratio.
Cancelled/failed/cache-conditioned runs are not comparative timing evidence.

The original public Junk Shop was downloaded directly on the new server and
byte-matched to the preserved local original: 412,530,532 bytes, SHA
`33191670ef370dcaa5fa2483c7d75abc9b8ce106ecea33cd155dbfc95ff649d2`.
It is unchanged and prepared for the next heavy-scene gate; this phase did not
render that scene. Next work should first instrument render/publish/SDK/display
latency and validate CUDA dimension-only film reuse in coherent mode, then expand
guides/motion against original heavy materials. Do not reuse software-GL numbers
as a workstation performance result or weaken the existing heavy gate.

## Phase 5: actual path features and reflected/environment motion

This is a bounded implementation/validation milestone. The full renderer/addon,
final still/animation/SR, heavy-scene quality and responsiveness goal remains
**active**. No defaults, marketing claims, commit, push or release are promoted.
Evidence is `dev-tools/reviews/2026-10-16/dlss/phase5/`.

### Implementation and accuracy boundary

Only explicit coherent RTPATH `SLC_DENOISING_FRAMES` uses guide semantic version2.
Ordinary rendering and legacy TILEPATH primary-guide version1 stay unchanged.
Features follow the **already sampled eye path**, including glass, GGX roughglass,
mirror, supported OpenPBR transmission and environment termination. No added ray,
BSDF sample, random dimension, roulette decision or canonical transport weight is
introduced. Deferred directional-albedo, weighted normal and path-depth features
are auxiliary reconstruction inputs, with the smooth GGX alpha split and small
feature cutoff; they are not a new unbiased beauty estimator or quality proof.
Specular albedo/roughness remain first-boundary features. Unsupported vertices
remain sticky status2; missing samples and unsupported whole frames are rejected.

The existing 32-float record is retained. Reserved fields carry guide version,
deferred features, motion point/kind and first reflector shading normal. Primary
world positions remain actual renderer hit positions. A finite reflected point
is mirrored across the first shading-normal plane; reflected environment motion
mirrors the actual ray direction. Environment projection uses homogeneous w0 so
camera translation cannot create background motion. Diffuse-only reflection
produces no irrelevant specular motion/history reset. Array projection supports
both flat Nx3 and image HxWx3 inputs. Previous accepted frame matrices remain
paired across dropped publications; dimensions/cuts reset history.

Pinned Blender5.3 source `5d8151335b6b` uses `mis_origin_n = sd->N`. The followup
therefore corrected the initial geometric-normal model to **shading normal** and
added a tilted-normal analytical/native test. OpenPBR SSS remains explicitly
rejected until exit-point guides exist. Volumes/hair, unsupported mixed/translucent
closures, dispersion/thin-film, shadow catchers/holdouts, moving shutter meshes
and other incomplete guides are not fabricated or silently promoted.

Top-level frame `version` remains1 while `guide_version` distinguishes semantics.
Current native and adapter must be installed together. Compatibility with an
older adapter that ignores guide_version is an **open packaging/versioning gate**.
These private development wheels/profile tests are not standalone addon ZIP proof.

## Phase 6: finish active eye samples and cancel before the RTPATH barrier

Full goal remains **active**. This phase fixes an eye-sample completion bug and
its cancellation boundary. It does not complete general materials, heavy-scene
DLSS, final/animation/SR, quality, performance or standalone packages.
Evidence: `dev-tools/reviews/2026-10-16/dlss/phase6/`.

### Confirmed bug and implementation

Tile GPU rendering used `maxDepth*2-1` (plus optional state passes) as a fixed
trace budget. Transparent intersections, shadow continuations and subsurface
steps do not necessarily advance BSDF depth. The next tile Init could discard a
valid unsplatted eye path. On real CUDA,20 null planes at depth2 left **all3072
pixels missing in each of3 frames**; the zero-plane control was complete.

GPUTaskStats now distinguishes a valid active eye sample from padding/inactive
and cycling light tasks. Init resets statistics before every early return. After
the depth-based initial batch, the host reads actual sample completion and runs
batches of8 until active eye samples have splatted. This is shared by TILEPATHOCL
and RTPATHOCL, including partial edge tiles and sparse ordinary RTPATH. Film sample
counts are not filled or adjusted; transport weights, depth limits and canonical
sampling rules are unchanged. Previously discarded path work now actually runs.
This specifically drains **eye** samples; it is not proof for every other
estimator/lighting configuration or all subsurface/volume behavior.

A second lifecycle issue follows from an unbounded completion drain: RTPATH
requests the jthread stop token only after its frame barrier. Stop/edit/resize
therefore need an earlier cancellation signal. A dedicated per-thread atomic
signal now interrupts both the initial batch and drain; Stop, scene edit and
film resize signal all devices before the barrier. Camera updates clear it after
buffer updates, and restarted threads reset it at entry. Cancelled frames remain
incomplete and are rejected by the strict consumer. No synthetic guide or sample
count is used to conceal cancellation.

With32768 null planes in **one merged mesh**, old completion candidate A took
4.2249217s to Stop, violating the2s functional deadline. Current B took0.0062717s
on CUDA and0.0150065s on Metal. These are bounded lifecycle checks, not renderer
speed ratios or a general responsiveness claim. An earlier fixture with32768
separate meshes and a Python log callback timed out before GPU allocation; it is
inconclusive for Stop and excluded. Its own process was reaped. The replacement
records explicit setup/start/stop stages and uses ordinary native logging.

## Phase 7 — Lambert 반투명 가이드와 원본 GenmaB 실제 DLSS

전체 목표는 계속 진행 중이다. 현재 단계는 원본 GenmaB를 SuperLuxCore에서
DLSS로 처리하는 RGB 진단 경로를 열었으며, 전체 파이널·애니메이션·SR 지원이나
실무 반응성/품질 게이트를 통과한 결과가 아니다. 기존 Cycles 비교와 구별한다.

CPU 및 실제 GPU `MatteTranslucent` 가이드는 기존 재질의 실제 hit-point 알베도
`clamp(Kr) + clamp(Kt) * (1 - clamp(Kr))`를 사용한다. 두 Lambert 성분은 모두
확산이므로 diffuse albedo, roughness 1 및 실제 shading normal을 기록한다.
추가 BSDF 샘플, 난수 차원, transport weight/default 변경이나 미지원 픽셀 채우기는
없다. rough translucent, mix, SSS 출구, 볼륨/헤어 등은 이 지원으로 일반화하지 않는다.

현재 지문:

- macOS native `f971553b19d46e69a99fe86493aaf75ac0717ce6e8ea70560df35b4fad2a6943`
- macOS wheel `18f62f6a1288517f84cddc979d00e33a7922506984388fce233927c3b65b8e2e`
- CUDA native `7f16c1b8d96e2e2ef19f9bd57f6f2ffe417a2e6b58c5f69788be5e83999d7d13`
- Linux wheel `7e7a72f2435d482ec4f49c2c2ff57055a9d943004ff9a08842825376879f3e81`
- 공유 CL 181개 tree `aee26db48b70dfcdb362b348d1e61b1d4b0cc84ee310e167e028893ee1d799e1`
- 표시 어댑터 `8e1ede6e183b6af7d52836cde76ac7ba03b641741a4cc7499e695a40789de3db`

13개 fixture의 동일 frame ID/jitter 원시 RGB 비교는 CUDA 523쌍, Metal 299쌍이
`array_equal`을 통과했다. CUDA의 실제 NGX 평가 136회와 camera 32회/resize 16회,
CPU/CUDA host primary guide 각 13케이스, Metal 경로/CPU host guide 및 720p
legacy GPU 회귀를 통과했다. CPU Lambert 투과 720p 및 현재 Metal 720p 캡처를
원시 32-bit EXR/ACES 2.0 PNG로 내보내 직접 확인했고 EXR 재개방 오차는 0이다.
CPU/Metal에서 NVIDIA DLSS를 실행했다는 의미는 아니다. CPU preview는 어둡고,
Metal 저샘플 이미지는 강하게 노이즈가 남아 있어 beauty 품질 통과로 취급하지 않는다.
깊은 투명 경로 Stop은 현재 CUDA 약0.00482초, Metal 약0.01368초로 각 2초 기능
기준을 통과했다. 이 값은 렌더링 성능비가 아니다.

원본 GenmaB의 기존 320x180 첫 프레임 미지원 1,441픽셀이 현재 RGB 진단에서는
사라졌다. 320p 8장과 1280x720 32장이 모두 실제 GPU 가이드 status1, pixel당
sample_count1, complete 및 finite 검사를 통과했고 실제 NGX 평가를 수행했다.
원본 geometry/materials/lights/Camera.003와 Khronos PBR Neutral을 유지하며,
RGB eye-path/depth25와 cache/LT/MNEE/VC/guiding/spectral 진단 override는
각 config/report에 명시한다. 원본 파일은 저장하지 않았고 SHA256 f6305983… 그대로다.
원시 평균과 DLSS 비교는 정확히 같은 32개 accepted 입력을 사용하되 재구성 가중치는
상이하다. 고샘플 정답/동일오차 성능/전체 원본 설정의 파이널 검증이 아니다.
노이즈는 크게 줄지만 유리의 잔여 노이즈와 바닥·그림자 평활화가 보인다.
DLSS 평균 RGB는 같은 32입력 평균보다 약5.23/3.86/2.94% 낮다. 노이즈가 있는
32-SPP 평균을 정답으로 두어 bias/품질을 확정하지 않는다.

원본의 서로 다른 Blender 내보내기 RGB 완전일치는 통과하지 못했다. 새 대조에서
이전 B/B 반복도306픽셀, 현재 C/C 반복도296픽셀이 달랐으며 primary positions와
sample counts는 정확히 같다. 단일 B/C 차이만으로 새 회귀/보존 어느 쪽도 확정할 수
없고, exporter/device order/kernel 중 무엇이 원인인지 아직 증명되지 않았다.
원본 strict preservation은 열린 게이트로 유지한다. 기존 fixed text export의64+64
비교를 원본과 동등하다고 바꾸지 않는다. binary Save unregistered-class와 text
roundtrip 위치/재질 차이도 계속 미해결이다.

실제 원본 viewport의 HDR upload는 half float에서 max abs0.00202179로 기존
0.002 기준에 실패했다. Blender GPU API가 RGB32F를 제공하지 않아 초기 시도는
실패했고, 지원되는 RGBA32F와 opaque alpha1로 수정했다. 새 pixels일 때만 pack/upload
한다. opaque DLSS texture는6→16bytes/pixel이며 한 번의 임시 RGBA 배열이 필요하다.
원시 film/SDK 입력은 수정하지 않는다. 원본 v4의 controlled GPU draw 오차0.0003112는
동일0.002 기준을 통과한다. 단순 live-region one-pixel oracle은 약0.0989로 실패한
별도 진단이며 controlled pass와 혼합하지 않는다.

원본 heavy viewport v4는 **150초 기준 실패**다. orbit 새 프레임3회가
15.326/25.722/14.406초에 표시됐고,4번째는 미완료이며 resize에는 도달하지 못했다.
반응성 개선이나 hardware-display 성능으로 주장하지 않는다. 별도 toy 실제 Blender
검사는4 orbit/2 resize, runtime 누락 ordinary fallback→복구, 동일 native session
유지와 worker 회수를 통과했다. 실제 CUDA/NGX지만 GL은 Xvfb/llvmpipe이다.
현재 원본/fixture 측정으로 속도비를 계산하지 않는다.

다음 작업은 원본의 render→frame publication→lock/readback/NumPy→SDK→native PP
→upload/draw 단계 지연을 계측하고, 기존4 orbit/2 resize·150초 및 이미지 기준을
유지하면서 병목을 해결하는 것이다. 이후 고샘플/동일오차 품질, 실제 object/deformation
motion과 파이널·애니메이션·SR/AOV/alpha/color·standalone ZIP/Windows 지원을
완료해야 한다. top frame version1/guide_version2의 구형 어댑터 호환 위험도 남아 있다.

증거는 workspace `dev-tools/reviews/2026-10-16/dlss/phase7/phase7-report.json`,
`checkpoint.json`, `comparison/` 및 `evidence/evidence-manifest.json`이다. 이미지/로그/JSON
138개를 개별 SHA256으로 검증했다. 약1,182,470,596bytes의 이전 profile/불일치
capture를 정리했다. prefix 정리 실수로 선택된 matched Metal 파일116개는 삭제 전
전체 file SHA256과 동일한 사본으로 즉시 복구했으며299쌍을 다시 확인했다.
현재 profile은 다음 baseline용으로 유지하고 고유 old/current wheel과 원본·일치 raw·
EXR/PNG·실패 로그는 보존한다. 서버 receipt PID21개가 모두 사라졌고 자체 task/GPU
process 잔류가 없다. commit/push/release 및 기본 Blender 설치 변경은 없다.


## Phase8 — GenmaB 병목 계측, 후보 기각 및 제품 복구 (2026-10-17)

원본 GenmaB를 실제 Blender UI → CUDA → NGX → view_draw 경로로 계측했다.
제품 바이너리7f16/기존 adapter를 쓰는 전체 검사는 기존4 orbit/2 resize·150초 및
controlled upload max0.002 기준을 유지했다. orbit4회는 각각14.180/14.304/14.037/
24.609초였고 첫 resize에서150초 제한에 실패했다. worker와 자식은 모두 회수했다.
controlled GPU upload 오차0.00029975는 통과했지만 단순 live-region oracle의
0.10137 실패는 별도 열린 항목이다. 서버 GL은 llvmpipe이며 하드웨어 디스플레이
반응성/성능비로 해석하지 않는다.

native 프레임 공개 간격은 약12초다. GetDenoisingFrame은 이미 read-only lease로
큰 배열의 consumer 복사를 피한다. 완료 시각은 presentation context 복제 직전의
gettimeofday이며 소비 시 관찰한 차이는 약0.10–0.27초였다. adapter 준비는 정적
약0.4–0.5초/카메라 변경 약1.2초, SDK는 초기 이후 대개0.1–0.15초, native PP는
약0.02–0.06초, 카메라 EndSceneEdit은 약0.6–0.8초다. 이들은 계측 진단이며
완전한 cold-start/동일오차 성능비가 아니다.

기존 물리 설정을 낮추지 않은 private CUDA event 계측은64 launch당1개,
최대256 pending event pair를 사용했다. 첫 D1 bfb918e2의 전체 UI 검사는 실패했고
Blender 종료 전 device 소멸자가 실행되지 않아 CSV가 없어 timing 근거로 쓰지
않는다. D2 2c31ca9f는 동기 readback 시 최대1Hz flush를 추가했다. 정적 upload2회와
orbit1회의 짧은 진단만 통과했으며 기존 전체 검사를 대체하지 않는다.11,753회 중
184표본의 외삽 GPU 시간에서 VC_CONNECT 약21.084초, LIGHT_VERTEX 약20.725초가
가장 컸다. Optix 추적 약0.886초와 host enqueue는 상대적으로 작았다. 이벤트가
실행에 영향을 주므로 제품 ABBA/속도 개선율은 계산하지 않는다. 원래 VC와 LT의
의존 관계, depth25, MNEE 등 실제 export 설정을 유지했다.

NumPy 투영의 einsum 후보5be7d0b7는 큰 compact921,600점의 point 투영만 보면
중앙값0.19588→0.10352초였지만, image 배열0.06866→0.10312초와 compact230,400점
0.01019→0.02712초로 회귀했다. 각 점을1x3 batched matmul로 만드는 두 번째 후보도
작은 배열과 방향 투영에 회귀했다. 하드웨어/배열 크기 heuristic이나 전역 BLAS 설정을
추가하지 않고 둘 다 기각했다. 후보 소스와 수치/시간 로그는 phase8/candidates 및
각 bench JSON에 남겼다. 후보 각각 macOS의 projection2/motion5/history3 검사를
통과했고 einsum은 Linux의 같은10검사 및 실제 CUDA13케이스/NGX136회,
camera32회/resize16회를 통과했다. 이것은 기각한 후보의 한정된 검증이다.
복구한 현재 원본 adapter는 새 projection2 검사를 통과했다. 시스템 Python에 numpy가
없었던 첫 실행 실패도 기록하고 기존 Blender5.2 Python3.13으로 재실행했다.

제품 source에는 CUDA profiler를 채택하지 않았다. remote cudadevice.cpp와
optixaccelhw.cpp 두 파일을 exact before로 복구했다. 최초 copy2가 옛 mtime을 보존해
Ninja가 재빌드를 생략한 사실을 바이너리 SHA로 발견했고, 원본 source hash 확인 후
mtime을 갱신해 강제 재빌드했다. 최종 module은 기존 제품 SHA7f16c1b8…와 정확히
일치하며 wheel/profile도 같다. macOS native f971553b…/wheel18f62f6a…,
adapter7e103118… 및181개 CL tree aee26db4…도 현재 다시 확인했다. 이전 Phase7
CPU/Metal/Blender 검증을 새 계측 빌드의 통과로 바꾸지 않는다. 제품 코드 변경은
없고 새 projection oracle 회귀 테스트만 남겼다.

실제 SuperLuxCore 비교 이미지는 phase7/comparison의 원본1280x720, 같은32개
accepted 입력, no-upscale, Camera.003/KhronosPBRNeutral 결과다. 원본 geometry/
materials/lights를 보존하되 RGB eye-path/depth25 및 cache/LT/MNEE/VC/guiding/
spectral 진단 override를 명시한다. 현재 다시 이미지 SHA와 원본 blend f6305983…를
확인하고 전체/100% crop을 직접 검수했다. 노이즈 감소, 유리 잔여 노이즈, 바닥·그림자
평활화가 보인다. 고샘플 정답·동일오차 속도·파이널/애니메이션 전체 품질 증거는 아니다.

완료된 격리 profiler profile과 기각 후보의 중복 코드200,134,180bytes를 제거했다.
고유 D1/D2 wheel, source patch, CSV, 로그, JSON과 raw/이미지/원본은 보존했다.
증거87개/2,385,329bytes를 개별 SHA256으로 전송·검증했고 서버 receipt의 소유 PID,
GPU job과 macOS 자체 프로세스 잔류가 없다. 증거 기준 파일은
workspace dev-tools/reviews/2026-10-16/dlss/phase8/phase8-report.json,
evidence/evidence-manifest.json, macos-final-audit.json이다.

다음 단위는 VC/LT의 task-domain 불변식을 확인하고 해당 기능을 켠 결정적 baseline
fixture를 추가한 뒤 큰 두 커널의 좁은 최적화를 검증하는 것이다. 기존13 fixture는
LT/VC를 꺼서 이 변경의 충분한 회귀 검사가 아니다. 아직 lane-domain 최적화를 적용하지
않았다. 전체 heavy150초 검사, 고샘플/동일오차/ghosting, 원본 strict RGB 재현성,
파이널/애니메이션/SR/AOV/alpha/color, 복잡한 재질·object/deformation/shutter/lens
motion과 standalone Linux/Windows 배포는 계속 열린 Goal이다. commit/push/release,
사용자 기본 Blender 설치 및 물리 추정기/기본 렌더 품질 설정 변경은 없다.


## Phase9 — LT/VC 실행 범위 후보 검증·기각 및 원복 (2026-10-17)

Phase8의 큰 두 커널에 대해 dense 실행 범위를 줄이는 후보를 만들었다. InitGPUTask의
광원 작업은 [eyeTaskCount,taskCount) tail에서 MK_LIGHT_INIT으로 시작하며 광원
scheduler/adjoint SSS가 같은 gid를 유지한다. 눈 SSS는 adjoint=false이고 VC는
EyePathInfo를 사용한다. 이 불변식을 확인하고 LIGHT_INIT/LIGHT_VERTEX의 gid를
원래 tail index로 재매핑하면서 lightTaskCount개만 호출하고, VC는 eyeTaskCount개만
호출했다. wavefront queue mapping, seed, 경로 수/깊이, 연결 가중치와 물리 설정은
바꾸지 않았다. 그러나 실제 성능 이득이 확인되지 않아 **제품에는 채택하지 않았다**.

후보는 Mac native0763ecb7…/wheel e1e35020… 및 Linux native d97e5765…/wheel
7f759527…이고 CL181개 tree06086302…이다. 이 값은 보존한 후보 wheel과 검증 증거의
지문이며 현재 설치 지문으로 쓰지 않는다. remote host CPP는 플랫폼별 기존 차이가 있어
첫 whole-file hash 사전 검사에서 중단했다. 정확히 같은 EnqueueAdvancePathsKernel
함수만 치환해 나머지 WIP를 유지했다. 첫 Mac sync의 base wheel 누락 및 첫 heavy
사전 검사의 구 프로필 assertion도 실행 전에 수정했고 실패 로그를 남겼다.

새 dlss_light_task_domains_test.py는 LT/VC를 실제로 켠 RT 세 조건(광원 비율25% 및
75%), TILEPATHOCL, PATHOCL dense/queued, light-only7케이스를 검증한다. CUDA와
Metal 모두 통과했다. RT 세 조건의24장씩72장에서는 guides/sample counts가
정확히 일치하고 LT24장의 RGB가 완전히 같다. VC RGB 최대 차이1.1920928955078125e-7는
기존 native의 자기 반복에서도 나타났다. 원시 RGB 원본의 보편적 보존/품질을 이 사실로
확정하지 않는다. 기존 엄격한 eye-only 검사의 허용 오차도 낮추지 않았다.

후보의 기존13경로 strict RGBA는 CUDA466쌍, Metal314쌍이 array_equal을 통과했다.
실제 NGX136회 및 camera32/resize16, host CPU/CUDA 각13케이스와 Mac CPU/Metal
각13케이스, Metal720p legacy GPU 가이드를 통과했다. Metal32768겹 투명 경로 Stop은
0.01321초로 기존2초 기능 기준을 통과했다. 현재 후보의 CPU/Metal720p raw EXR와
ACES2도 내보내 재개방 오차0을 확인하고 직접 검수했다. CPU 이미지는 매우 어둡고
Metal 저샘플 beauty는 강한 노이즈가 남아 있다. beauty 품질 완료나 CPU/Metal의
NVIDIA DLSS 실행으로 보고하지 않는다. 이 결과는 기각한 후보에 대한 증거다.

GPU 및 다른 render/build 작업의 PID를 검사한 A–B–B–A4회는 모두 완료/회수됐고
관찰된 경합이 없었다. 동일96x64 scene/settings의 각 조건64장 중 처음8장을 제외한
56연속 프레임 간격만 사용했다. LT는6.302→6.448ms, VC는29.851→30.050ms,
75% 광원 VC는113.552→114.106ms였다. 후보 평균 지연이 각각2.31/0.67/0.49%
길어 이득을 입증하지 못했다. 확정적인 일반 회귀율로 확대하지 않으며, SDK·원본
GenmaB·뷰포트·cold-start·고샘플/동일오차 성능은 이 소형 측정 범위 밖이다.

실제 원본 GenmaB는 기존4orbit/2resize·150초 및 controlled upload0.002 기준을
유지했다. orbit은25.439/14.338/14.211/13.712초였고 첫 resize에서150초 제한에
실패했다. native.Start 약2.69초로 이미 warm 상태여서 cold compilation을 핑계로
다시 실행하거나 기준을 늘리지 않았다. controlled draw 오차0.00030583은 통과했고,
단순 live-region oracle0.09561 실패는 별도 항목이다. 외부 Blender가 관찰됐으므로
이 heavy 결과로 속도비를 계산하지 않는다. Xvfb/llvmpipe도 workstation GL 검증을
대체하지 않는다. Blender child의 exit0은 failed result를 저장하고 종료한 것이며
하네스 통과가 아니다. outer supervisor는 실패를 보고했다. 모든 자체 worker/display/
process가 회수됐고120,183,663bytes quit.blend 임시파일도 하네스에서 제거했다.

후보 세 파일만 before/after hash를 확인해 원복하고 양쪽 모두 강제 재빌드했다.
원래 Mac native f971553b…와 Linux7f16c1b8…, CL tree aee26db4…가 정확히 복원됐다.
원본 phase7 profile/wheel, adapter7e103118… 및 다른 WIP는 보존했다. Mac 복구 wheel은
보존한 원본 wheel과 모든 ZIP member의 SHA가 같음을 확인한 뒤 중복본만 제거했다.
프로필/중복 wheel 정리는 합계600,670,509bytes이며 quit.blend 정리는 이 합계 밖이다.
고유 후보 wheel, 패치/before/after, raw/PNG/EXR/CSV/JSON/로그는 남겼다.
증거125개/3,500,962bytes를 개별 SHA로 전송·검증했고 server receipt PID18개가
모두 사라졌으며 Mac/서버의 자체 native/GPU 프로세스 잔류가 없다.

다음 단위는 파이널 렌더의 coherent sample interval/누적/history/output 계약이다.
현재 engine/final.py에는 DLSS 실행 경로가 없고 final denoiser enum도 DLSS를 노출하지
않는다. 누적 필름을 독립1sample로 가장하거나 가이드/alpha/AOV를 만들어 채우지 않고
실제 renderer-owned 입력을 사용해야 한다. 이후 실제 Blender F12/애니메이션/SR 연결과
고샘플/동일오차, 복잡한 재질·object/deformation/shutter/lens motion, 원본 strict 재현성,
heavy 반응성, standalone Linux/Windows 배포 게이트를 모두 완료해야 한다. full Goal은
active이며 제품 속도/품질 기본값·사용자 기본 Blender 설치·commit/push/release 변경은 없다.

증거: workspace dev-tools/reviews/2026-10-16/dlss/phase9/phase9-report.json,
evidence/evidence-manifest.json, abba-summary.json 및 macos-restored-cleanup.json.


## 2026-10-17 Phase10 — coherent 파이널 렌더의 제한된 실제 통합

이번 단위는 progress다. native 프레임 전달과 실제 stock Blender F12/애니메이션
연결을 구현했지만 전체 100% 지원 Goal은 active로 유지한다. 기본 denoiser는 OIDN이며,
새 항목은 명시 선택하는 “NVIDIA DLSS Ray Reconstruction (Experimental)”이다.

기존 PublishDenoisingFrame의 canonical AddFilm을 정확히 한 번 재사용해 원시 RGB와
모든 AOV를 누적한다. 실제 새1SPP 입력만 RR에 전달하고, 누적 beauty를 독립 sample로
가장하지 않는다. 선택적인 lossless 모드는 정확한 lease를 ACK할 때까지 다음 프레임을
대기한다. 오래된/다른 lease는 거부하고, ACK를 보류해도 Stop/edit/resize는 진행된다.
기존 viewport latest 모드가 기본이며 이번에 CL181개는 수정하지 않았다.

파이널 presentation context만 canonical 누적 필름에서 복제하고 DENOISED/Combined로
내보낸다. 원시 필름이나 AOV를 neural 값으로 덮지 않는다. 실제 F12에서 halt export가
config 뒤에 합쳐져 요청4입력이5개 처리되는 오류를 찾았다. 실제 halt properties를
configure_producer에 전달하고 독립 모드 전용 exact SPP cap을 사용하도록 고쳤다.
일반 Film의 > halt 규칙은 변경하지 않는다. 프레임/레이어마다 새 backend/history를
만들며 최종 draw 전에 raw/AOV를 함께 freeze하고 finally에서 backend/session을 회수한다.

현재 native는 Mac a2105fb6…, Linux28f71c45…이고 CUDA helper58467c2d…/ABI1은
그대로다. CL181개는 보존한 phase7 각 파일 SHA와 같고 tree aee26db4…다.
현재 wavefront/viewport worker와 SDK adapter도 기존 지문을 보존했다. 설치·테스트·
source/wheel/kernel의 전체 지문은 phase10/source-identity.json, macos-identity.json,
evidence/identity-final.json에 기록했다. Linux는 전용 driver/compiler 경로가 필요한
개발 profile이며 독립 배포 ZIP의 통과로 취급하지 않는다.

native slow-consumer8프레임은 CUDA/Metal에서 연속 ID/정확한 canonical8SPP를
통과했다. 실제 NGX8회 후에도 raw10채널이 변하지 않았고 matched control10종 모두
array_equal이었다. ACK 보류 중 edit/resize/오래된 lease 거부/Stop 및 latest 전환도
통과했다. Stop은 CUDA0.01618초, Metal0.00454초로2초 기능 기준 안이었다. 성능비가 아니다.

stock Blender5.3beta5d8151335b6b의 실제 F12는1280x720/8입력에서 NGX8회,
DENOISED=Combined=실제 NN presentation과 저장 EXR 재개방 오차<1e-6을 통과했다.
동일 native sampler의 control과 RGB/ALPHA/ALBEDO/AVG_SHADING_NORMAL/DEPTH/
POSITION/SAMPLECOUNT/DIRECT_DIFFUSE/EMISSION/OBJECT_ID10종 raw는 모두 정확히
같았다. fixture는 RGB eye-only/depth6/seed131/불투명 diffuse이며 선언한 진단 설정이다.
실제2프레임 애니메이션은 각4NGX/첫 입력 reset, 물체 위치 변화, 실제 저장 EXR과
captured Combined의 일치도 통과했다. shutter 안의 변형/물체 이력 품질 증거는 아니다.

투명 배경은 coverage를 opaque alpha로 바꾸지 않고 실제 기존 OIDN으로 전환했다.
SDK평가0회, 실제 raw alpha=Combined alpha와 EXR 저장을 확인했다. 기본 CUDA OIDN,
Mac stock5.2.1LTS의 CPU/Metal OIDN 및 Metal에서 DLSS 선택 후 OIDN 전환도 통과했다.
일반 OIDN 엔진의 target SPP overshoot는 허용된 기존 동작이며, 이를 exact coherent
8SPP와 성능/샘플 등가로 비교하지 않는다. BCD 전체 경로는 이번 단위에서 실행하지 않았다.

기존13경로의 strict raw 기준은 CUDA514쌍/Metal343쌍 array_equal을 통과했다.
실제 NGX136회 및 camera32/resize16, 각 CPU/CUDA/Metal host13케이스와 Metal720p
primary 가이드도 통과했다. Metal32768겹 투명 경로 Stop0.01297초는2초 기능 기준을
통과했다. CPU/Metal720p raw EXR/ACES2 재개방 오차0을 확인하고 직접 보았다. CPU
가이드 beauty는 매우 어둡고 Metal1SPP beauty는 강한 노이즈가 남아 있다. 새 CUDA
F12는 큰 노이즈 감소와 위치/경계를 보존하지만 표면/그림자에 저주파 얼룩이 남아 있어
고샘플/동일오차 품질 완료로 보고하지 않는다. 병렬 correctness로만 실행했고 속도비는 없다.

검증 하네스 오류도 숨기지 않았다. Mac 첫 F12는 Blender 시작 후 profile 변수를
설정해 기본 애드온을 선택했고 cache wheel/manifest/자체 설치 metadata가 바뀌었다.
해당 실행은 제외했다. 보관된 원래 SuperLuxCore2.11.34 공식 ZIP과 나머지511개
파일의 일치를 확인한 뒤 cache/manifest를 복구하고 이번 metadata를 제거했다. 복구
후 설치의513개 member SHA가 원본 ZIP과 같다. 기본 native 라이브러리는 쓰지 않았다.
사전 installation-info snapshot은 없어 그 metadata의 사전 바이트 복원을 주장하지 않는다.
환경 변수는 이제 Blender 시작 전에 적용하고 addon enable 전 repository.module/path,
enable 후 addon/native 경로를 검사한다. ps 권한 실패·잘못된 Mac Python 버전·구형
export 경로의 지문 실패도 각각 제외하고 수정 후 별도 통과 receipt를 남겼다.

요청된 GenmaB 이미지는 phase7의 실제 SuperLuxCore/RTX5060Ti720p 비교를 다시
직접 확인했다. 같은32개의 완전한 독립1SPP 입력, 업스케일링 없음, 원본 재질/조명/
Camera.003과 KhronosPBRNeutral이며 RGB eye-path/depth25/cache/LT/MNEE/VC/guiding/
spectral 진단 overrides를 명시한다. 벽·천장·기둥 노이즈 감소, 유리 잔여 노이즈와
바닥 디테일 smoothing이 보인다. 이 보존 이미지를 phase10 후보로 새로 렌더한 결과나
고샘플 정답·동일오차·속도 향상 증거로 바꾸지 않는다. 원본 blend SHA f6305983…는 유지됐다.

쓸모가 끝난 중간 v2 wheel은 Mac97,146,109/Linux46,323,160bytes 제거했다. 기각된
phase9의 raw 중 원본 phase7과 전체 파일 SHA가 같은 것만 remote466개429,525,248bytes,
Mac314개289,422,592bytes를 삭제하고 정확한 retained-path/SHA alias를 남겼다.
합계862,417,109bytes다. 현재 proof/raw/PNG/EXR/로그/JSON·고유 원본 wheel 및 다음
Goal 단위에서 쓸 현재 profile은 보존한다. server24개 receipt PID가 모두 없어졌고,
Mac/서버 자체 native/GPU/worker 잔류가 없다. 프로세스 조회는 사용 가능했다.

남은 필수 범위는 일반 alpha/coverage·SR·multi-SPP/adaptive, 복잡한 재질/볼륨/SSS/
반사굴절과 object/deformation/shutter/lens motion, 고샘플/동일오차·밝기/ghosting,
실제 heavy 장면의 최신 후보 orbit/resize/직접 GL 지연, 원본 strict 재현성/Save roundtrip,
CUDA resident interop 및 Linux/Windows standalone ZIP이다. 새 final loop의 기존200ms
polling과 AOV draw 비용을 측정·개선하고, 실행 중 halt/IP/light 설정 변경과 cancel/
device-loss도 최종 경로에서 검증해야 한다. Metal157초 전체 cold-start 개선은 미입증이다.
기존 WIP62파일과 CL181개 지문을 보존했고 commit/push/release나 품질 기본값 승격은 없다.

증거: workspace dev-tools/reviews/2026-10-16/dlss/phase10/phase10-report.json,
evidence/evidence-manifest.json, preservation.json, default-profile-restoration.json.


## 2026-10-17 Phase11/12 진행 — 파이널 루프 비용과 실행 중 종료 조건

Phase11은 progress다. 실제720p F12 관찰에서 SDK8회 upload/evaluate/readback 합계
약0.47초, 고정 loop sleep 약2.4초, 중간 전체 AOV draw 약3.5초가 관찰됐다. 최종
draw에 검사 배열/NPZ 저장 비용이 섞이는 것을 찾아 하네스가 capture 시간을 별도
기록하게 했다. 이 수치들은 좁은 단계 진단이며 전체 제품 속도비가 아니다.

NN 경로에만50ms native condition wait(GIL release)를 적용해 매 독립 입력의200ms
sleep을 없앴다. 입력이 없는 초기 반복 draw를 줄이고, 전체 AOV를 함께 유지하면서
초기 preview 주기를 제한했다. poll이 이미 갱신한 정확한 통계를 재사용하며 완료된
cap은 주기 통계까지 busy-spin하지 않는다. 기본 OIDN 루프와 transport/CL은 그대로다.
실제720p8-NGX/raw10-AOV/Combined/EXR, pause/resume, 취소 플래그 후 실제 raw필름
OIDN,2프레임 animation/alpha fallback 및 CPU/Metal 기본 F12를 통과했다.

320x180/32입력 A–B–B–A4회도 모두 완료됐다. 각 trial의 raw10채널 control 일치,
연속32SDK 입력 및 최종 출력 게이트를 통과했다. 서로 다른 process의 native9채널과
최종 Combined도 array_equal이다. 주소 기반 OBJECT_ID는 process 사이에 다르므로
그 항목은 각 trial 안에서만 strict 비교한다. B1/B2에 외부 Blender가 관찰되어 전체
성능 비교는 부적격이다. 관측 wall time으로 개선율을 만들지 않는다. 첫720p 후보도
외부 Blender가 겹쳐 성능비에서 제외했다. Phase11 당시 native는 Maca2105fb6/Linux
28f71c45였다. 코드/배열/receipt/관측은 phase11에 보존했다.

실제 F12에서 입력2 직후 샘플 목표8→16을 변경하니 controller가 OIDN으로 전환한 뒤
native의 독립 cap8이 남아 조기 종료했다. 새 회귀 하네스가 의도대로 실패했으며
통과 결과로 취급하지 않는다. Phase12에서 session film lock 아래 cap을 갱신하는
SetDenoisingFrameHaltSPP를 추가했다. Film→frame lock 순서를 유지하고 누적필름을
초기화하지 않는다. 파이널은 HALT 변경 때 실제 export.halt.convert 결과로 동기화한다.
RenderConfig에는 시작 batch 속성이 남으므로 그 값을 다시 읽지 않는다. 새 메서드의
존재를 확인해 구형 native에서는 OIDN으로 전환하며 조용히 잘못된 cap을 사용하지 않는다.

현재 Mac 후보e7bfc0d4…/wheel3b3c81ed…는 실제 Metal에서 완료된 cap4→8 재개,
고정8샘플과 raw10종 정확한 일치, cap 비활성화로9번째 입력, 보류 중인9샘플보다
낮은3으로 변경 후 추가 입력 없음, edit/resize/stale lease/Stop을 통과했다.
CPU/Metal host 각13케이스, 기존 strict Metal356쌍,720p guide/32768겹 Stop,
CPU/Metal OIDN 및 Metal의 DLSS 미지원 전환도 통과했다.720p rawEXR 재개방 오차0과
ACES2를 직접 검수했으나 CPU beauty는 어둡고 Metal1SPP는 노이즈가 많아 미관 품질
완료가 아니다. 이 결과는 NVIDIA 실행이나 최신 후보의 RTX 통과로 바꾸지 않는다.

RTX 서버 여유가3GiB 아래로 내려가 native patch dry-run 뒤 감독기가 중단했다.
dry-run exit0이어도 SSD error가 있어 빌드/패치 통과가 아니며 실제 source는 바뀌지
않았다. 기준을 낮추거나 다른 프로세스를 중단하지 않았다. 로컬에 전체 SHA 검증을
마친 Phase10 raw919,065,856bytes 및 Phase11 profile raw238,337,842bytes의
서버 중복본을 exact alias와 함께 evict했다. 이후에도 서버 여유가 줄어 오래된
Phase5/6/9 raw를 로컬로 스트리밍·개별 SHA 확인하며 옮기고 있다. 복사 중 .part와
미래 파일은 보존하고, 완전히 검증·rename된 prefix만 다시 서버 SHA를 확인해 evict한다.
살아 있는 전송을 중복 실행하지 않는다. 구체적인 현재 handle과 retry 조건은
phase12/checkpoint-in-progress.json에 기록한다. 원본 장면/고유 wheel/기준 Phase7
서버 raw와 무관한 WIP는 보존한다.

현재 full Goal은 active다. 최신 RTX native 빌드·완전 동기화·실제 live-halt8→16,
CUDA/NGX/720p/F12/animation/cancel 및 이전 strict 게이트는 아직 남았다. 그 뒤에도
전체 alpha/SR/재질·모션/고샘플·동일오차/heavy orbit·resize/standalone 배포 범위는
계속 필요하다. commit/push/release, 기본 denoiser/품질 승격이나 전체 성능 개선 주장은 없다.

증거: workspace dev-tools/reviews/2026-10-16/dlss/phase11/phase11-report.json,
abba-output-comparison.json, evidence/abba-summary.json 및 phase12/checkpoint-in-progress.json.


## 2026-10-17 Phase12 종료 검증 — 동적 목표에서도 DLSS 이력 유지

위 진행 기록의 미검증 RTX 항목을 최신 native로 완료했다. 현재 Mac native
e7bfc0d4…/원본 wheel3b3c81ed…, Linux native6a4c4030…/wheelef7e4542…다.
Linux LP64 Property 오버로드에 u_longlong 명시 변환을 적용해 빌드했고, 같은 수정의
Mac native와 모든 wheel member가 기존 e7bfc0 후보와 정확히 같음을 확인했다.
생성된97,146,445byte 중복 wheel은 지우고 private loader를 보존한 원본 wheel로 돌렸다.
별도 private Linux profile12를 만들어 구형 native에서 실제 OIDN/0SDK fallback을
먼저 확인한 뒤 새 SO/wheel/adapter를 함께 설치했다. 기존 profile7/10 Linux는 보존했다.

중요하게, HALT-only 변경은 neural history를 초기화하거나 OIDN으로 전환하지 않는다.
실제 Blender5.3beta/RTX5060Ti에서 입력2 직후8→16을 변경해 native16샘플, SDK16회,
첫 입력만 reset, fallback없음을 확인했다. 처음부터16으로 설정한 대조 렌더와
raw10채널이 array_equal이고 실제 NN→Combined→저장 EXR도 일치한다. 구형 실패를
재현한 결과와 최신 통과를 구분해 보존했다. 지원이 없는 native에서 조용히 잘못된
cap을 쓰는 대신 명시적으로 기존 OIDN을 사용하는 compatibility 경로도 실제 통과했다.

최신 native CUDA 515쌍/Metal 356쌍의 기존13경로 raw strict 비교,
실제 NGX136회/camera32/resize16, CPU/CUDA/Metal host 각13케이스, native8-input/
dynamic cap/lifecycle 및 Metal720p/deep32768-layer Stop을 통과했다. 실제720p8NGX
F12/raw10/Combined/EXR,2프레임4NGX animation과 저장 EXR, pause/resume,
취소 플래그 후 실제 frozen raw의 OIDN, alpha fallback와 기본 CPU/Metal/CUDA OIDN도
통과했다. 취소→최종 capture1.16397초는 실제 stop-flag 분기 기능 검사이며
workstation Esc/UI latency 측정은 아니다. 일반 OIDN의 샘플 overshoot를 NN의
exact8과 동등한 조건으로 비교하지 않는다. 최신720p/animation에도 외부 build/render가
있었으므로 어떤 속도비도 만들지 않는다.

최신720p NN/raw, 움직인2번째 animation 및 transparent fallback PNG를 직접 봤다.
NN은 노이즈가 크게 줄지만 표면/그림자 저주파 얼룩이 남아 있다. CPU/Metal720p
EXR 재개방 오차0·ACES2도 검수했다. CPU 이미지는 매우 어둡고 Metal1SPP는 노이즈가
강하므로 guide/numerical 증거다. 고샘플/동일오차 또는 전체 재질·모션 품질 완료가 아니다.

서버 원시 증거의 중복본만 개별 SHA로 로컬에 보존한 뒤 evict했다. Phase10/11과
완료된 Phase5/6/9 streams의 exact alias 합계4,630,674,666bytes다. 원본 scene,
unique wheel 및 기준 Phase7 raw는 서버에 남겼다. 진행 중 .part/future 파일은 건드리지
않았고 전송 완료 후 남은 mirror도 별도 receipt로 정리했다. 이번 증거
1082개/1098113075bytes를 모두 재해시했고 partial은 없다. 서버20개 감독 receipt PID가 모두
없어졌고 Mac/서버 자체 native/GPU/worker 잔류가 없다. 다른 작업은 중단하지 않았다.
원래 blend SHA f6305983…/기존 별도 WIP55파일/CL181개(aee26db4…)를 보존했다.
기본 Blender의513개 배포 member도 이전 복구 상태와 일치하며 이번에는 바꾸지 않았다.

이 단위는 progress이며 full Goal은 active다. 다음 우선 작업은 고샘플 기준/기준 자체
variance/동일오차의 정량·시각 검증이다. 최신 후보의 실제 GenmaB/JunkShop 무거운
장면 반응성, 전체 alpha/SR/4K memory budget·adaptive, 복잡한 재질/반사굴절/
object·deformation·shutter·lens motion, live IP/light/device-loss, CUDA resident interop
및 standalone Linux/Windows ZIP도 필요하다. 기존 원본 strict 재현성/Save roundtrip과

## 2026-10-17 Phase13 — 불투명 출력의 coverage와 별도 alpha AOV 분리

최신 후보로 원본 GenmaB720p 고샘플 검증을 시작하자, 기존 FinalDLSS가
독립 입력의 alpha!=1을 모두 거부해 첫 입력부터 OIDN으로 전환했다. 이 원본의
실제 transparent_film 선택은 false다. 기존 RGB Combined는 sky/glass coverage
AOV와 관계없이 alpha1이므로, 이 제한은 불투명 출력까지 잘못 막았다.

FinalDLSS에 실제 transparent_film 선택을 전달한다. 불투명 출력은 실제 NN RGB를
그대로 쓰고, native accumulated ALPHA가 있는 경우 해당 완성 prefix의 alpha를
presentation scratch에 읽어 image-pipeline 배경 합성에 전달한다. 최종 Combined의
alpha는 기존 opaque RGB 변환처럼1이다. native RGB/ALPHA/AOV와 raw packet은
수정하지 않는다. 투명 출력의 fractional coverage/premultiplication은 여전히 기존
OIDN으로 명시적으로 전환한다. 이를 일반 투명 DLSS 지원으로 승격하지 않는다.

실제 stock Blender5.3beta/RTX5060Ti에서 opaque-alpha8NGX(첫 입력만 reset),
transparent alpha0NGX/OIDN,720p8NGX final/Combined/EXR 게이트를 통과했다.
opaque fixture의57600픽셀 중49040픽셀이 alpha<1이어도 NN을 수행하며, 대조와
native10채널이 array_equal이다. Combined alpha는1, 별도 원시 alpha0..1은 보존했다.
현재 Python 어댑터로 Mac5.2.1 CPU OIDN/Metal OIDN/Metal DLSS선택→OIDN도 실제
통과했다. 새 opaque/transparent 및720p ACES2를 직접 확인했고 NN 저주파 얼룩은
남아 있다. 이 단위에서 native/CL/추정기/품질 기본값은 변경하지 않았다.

현재 engine/final SHA5ce4908a…/dlss_final SHA9dd55b78…, Linux native6a4c4030…,
Mac nativee7bfc0d4…, CL181/aee26db4…다. Linux는 독립 profile13에서 새 adapter를
검증한다. 원래 profile12의 긴 reference는 유지하며, .so/.whl만 immutable hardlink로
공유해 SSD 중복을 줄였다. default addon513 member/원본blend/별도WIP55는 재해시로
유지 확인했다. Phase12 원시 proof1072593720byte는 로컬의 개별SHA mirror를 검증한
뒤 서버 중복본만 제거했다. unique proof/원본/wheel은 지우지 않았다.

GenmaB는 RGB eye-path/depth25의 동일한 진단 설정으로 candidate32/64/128 및
별도 seed907/1907의512SPP reference를 준비한다. 일부 렌더는 진행 중이며 최종
receipt/이미지/두 reference 자체의 variance가 있어야 quality-metrics를 해석한다.
두512 reference도 ground truth가 아니며 공유된 scene/integrator 오류를 검증하지
못한다. 병렬 correctness 실행이므로 어떤 속도비도 만들지 않는다. 최신 source의
무거운 scene orbit/resize, 전체 alpha/SR/material/motion/standalone 배포는 미완료다.

DLSS5 질의는 공식 NVIDIA 연구/발표 및 nvpro-samples/vk_gltf_renderer의 NR 문서를
확인했다. NR은 기존 RR과 별개인 생성형 최종 외형 단계다. 공식 Vulkan 예제는
RR/SR→tonemap→LDR NR, depth/motion 및 material mask를 연결하며 NR전용 beta
NGX header가 있어야 빌드한다. 현재 보유 public header의 Feature13 RR/Reserved18과
실제6a4c+58467c RR 실행을 DLSS5 NR지원으로 부르지 않는다. RTX50 계열의 하드웨어
지원 발표와 현재 Linux/CUDA의 SDK/runtime/driver 실제 지원은 구분한다. NR을
연결할 때도 물리 raw32F/AOV와 선택형 외형 출력을 보존해야 한다.

공식 출처:
- https://research.nvidia.com/labs/adlr/DLSS5/
- https://www.nvidia.com/en-us/geforce/news/dlss-5-3d-guided-neural-rendering/
- https://github.com/nvpro-samples/vk_gltf_renderer/blob/master/docs/denoising.md
- https://github.com/nvpro-samples/vk_gltf_renderer/releases

opaque-alpha-preservation.json,preservation.json,source-identity.json,

## 2026-10-17 Phase14 — 투명 경계 누출 수정과 유한 고샘플 기준의 화질 한계

이 단위는 progress다. 기존 referenceB47507/PID278289를 중복 실행하지 않고
완료·reap했다. 실제 GenmaB128NN와 seed907/1907의512SPP 두 기준을 개별SHA로
로컬에 보존했고, 물리 config 차이는 seed와 coherent presentation/delivery3항목뿐이다.
원본 형상/재질/조명/Camera.003와 RGB eye-path/depth25 및 기존 이미지 resize 설정을
유지한 진단 조건이다. cache/LT/VC/MNEE/guiding/spectral은 명시적으로 껐다.
이는 원본 전체 설정·전체 재질·최종 beauty 통과가 아니다.

reference 평균은1024개 path sample을 포함하지만, native jitter는 frameID로만
정해져 두 시드가 같은512개 camera jitter 위치를 공유한다. 기준 차이로 추정한 noise는
그 camera schedule에 조건부인 path-RNG noise이며 shared quadrature/export/integrator
오류를 포함하지 않는다. geometry export 동일성도 config 비교만으로 입증하지 않는다.
기준 평균 자체의 fullRGB MSE noise 추정은0.0007946545다. ground truth나 신뢰구간으로
부르지 않는다. raw128 대 NN128의 기준 평균 대비 pixel MSE는0.006897735 대
0.002642364로 줄었다. 그러나 NN 평균 휘도는-2.5213%(ceiling-structure -3.4944%),
16×16 block평균 MSE는0.0004012432로 raw0.00002796679보다 크다. 화질 gate는
실패/미완료 상태로 유지한다. 고휘도 영역의 신호·가이드 및 visible emission/간접/반사를
분리 진단한 뒤 개선하며, 전역 노출 gain으로 수치를 맞추지 않는다. 속도비는 없다.

새 파이널 controller는 RGB만 복원하고 canonical accumulated ALPHA를 native
image-pipeline에 전달한다. 첫 허용형에서는 alpha0 픽셀의 NN RGB가 최대0.4539686
남았다. 실제 EXR의 alpha_mode는 PREMUL이므로 검정·흰 배경에서 밝은 halo가 된다.
완전히 covered된 픽셀은 NN RGB, alpha<1인 픽셀은 같은 완성 raw prefix의 실제 native
RGB를 사용하도록 수정했다. 추가 alpha 곱을 넣지 않고 원래 configured plugins를 유지한다.
원시 packet/film/10AOV는 불변이다. buffer는 재사용하며 final close에서 해제한다.
경계는 sampled RGB/noise를 유지하므로 joint RGB/alpha reconstruction 완료가 아니다.
얇은 투과·hair/volume/SSS 등 일반 투명 재질 품질까지 승격하지 않는다.

최신 helper b18b4366…/F12 harness615fa768…로 actual stock Blender5.3beta/RTX5060Ti
opaque-alpha8NGX, transparent720p8NGX,2-frame transparent animation 각8NGX,
opaque720p8NGX를 통과했다(현재 후보 총40NGX). transparent720p의 부분 coverage1315픽셀은
native RGB와 정확히 같고 alpha0의781206픽셀 RGB 누출은0이다. covered139079픽셀은
실제로 NN RGB를 사용한다. raw10는 대조와 array_equal, alpha는 정확한 native0..1이며,
저장한 정지/애니메이션3개 EXR 재개방 오차0, PREMUL metadata를 확인했다. 같은 원시
RGB/alpha의 v2/v3 before/after crop과 검정/흰 배경 ACES2를 직접 검수했다.8SPP 경계에는
원시 AA/noise가 남는다. Mac CPU OIDN/Metal OIDN/Metal DLSS선택→OIDN도 최신 helper와
같은 private 설치에서 실제 통과했다. native6a4c/e7bfc, runtime58467c, CL181/aee26은 불변이다.
이전 CUDA515/Metal356 native suite를 이번 Python 수정의 재실행으로 바꾸지 않는다.

불투명 fixed fixture의 Combined는 이전 helper9dd55와 새 helperb18에서 bit-exact다.
GenmaB 화질 자료 자체는 source13 opaque9dd의 실제128입력이며 source14 재렌더로
표기하지 않는다. 비교판의 세 이미지 모두 같은 stock5.2 OCIO ACES2/Neutral로 내보냈고
32F EXR 재개방 오차0이다. renderer는 stock5.3beta였다. crop은 원래 픽셀, halo판은
명시적4배 nearest 확대다. 노출 fitting/AI image generation은 없다.

처음 transparent v1 oracle은 configured NOP+LINEAR1에 없는 alpha 곱을 잘못 기대해
거부됐다. 이를 기존 동작대로 고친 v2 뒤, 독립 coverage audit이 실제 halo를 찾아 v3
생산 코드를 수정했다. 테스트를 약화시켜 halo를 통과시키지 않았고 v3는 부분 RGB/raw
동일성까지 검사한다. 서버 CPU alpha-audit-v3는3GiB SSD guard로 시작 직후-15 종료·reap돼
통과로 세지 않았다. 렌더를 반복하지 않고 완성된 SHA-verified v3 배열을 로컬에서 검증했다.

완료된 duplicate raw/PNG/EXR만 exact local alias를 확인한 뒤 서버에서 제거했다.
이번 turn의 실제 mirror eviction 합계545856960bytes이며, unique 증거는 모두 남겼다.
원본blend f6305983…, 별도WIP55파일, CL181, 기본 addon513 ZIP member가 동일하다.
current native/wheel 원본 및 private profiles는 full Goal의 후속 검증에 필요해 유지한다.
서버15개 receipt PID와 Mac12개 receipt PID는 absent/reaped이며 다른 GPU 작업은 중단하지
않았다.3GiB guard 및 timeout/RAM 제한을 낮추지 않았다. 병렬/경합에서 속도비를 만들지 않는다.

full Goal은 active다. 다음은 실제 GenmaB 고휘도/guide 보존 및 heavy orbit/resize를
우선하며, joint transparency/배경 image pipeline,4K·SR/adaptive/multiSPP, full
material/motion/lens/shutter, resident CUDA interop·device-loss·취소/메모리 회수,
standalone optional Linux/Windows ZIP과 원본 Save/strict 재현성도 필요하다.
Metal157초 전체 cold-start 개선은 입증되지 않았다. DLSS5 NR의 beta SDK/API/runtime
조사는 별도 질의이며 기존 RR 개발의 새 blocker로 만들지 않는다. 기본 OIDN·물리 품질
기본값·commit/push/release 승격은 없다.

## Phase15 — coherent interaction budget, CUDA resize reuse, physical AOV attribution (2026-10-17)

Current source: viewport `a110509bad00`, session worker `269d8bd32225`, final helper
`b18b4366d606`. Linux native `6a4c40302e7b`, Mac native `e7bfc0d40fa1`, optional
runtime `58467c2dd1bb` and all181 CL sources/tree `aee26db48b70` are unchanged.
Receipts, SHA manifests, full logs and source snapshots are in
`../../../../dev-tools/reviews/2026-10-16/dlss/phase15/` (workspace-relative:
`dev-tools/reviews/2026-10-16/dlss/phase15/phase15-report.json`). This is progress;
the original full final/animation/material/motion/SR/packaging goal remains active.

### Interaction policy and current actual heavy scene

Coherent frames require every pixel, so native `SetRuntimeResolutionReduction`
intentionally ignores sparse overrides. The previous2.5M-pixel film-scale threshold
left the1165x869 DLSS viewport at full sampling cost. The new opt-in DLSS policy
uses a temporary512k-pixel film budget (this viewport582x434;4K divisor4), holding
until a current-session/edit/size NN preview is uploaded. It then restores the
latest full region; a10s bounded fallback prevents permanent low resolution when
the producer stalls. A5s first candidate restored before setup/render completed
and failed the unchanged150s gate; its complete evidence is retained.

Resize uses a copied preview config, preserving exported full dimensions and
transport settings, then restores full size. Dimensions-only coherent CUDA config
edits use the existing native `Parse` film-edit path. The same session/scene and
DLSS consumer survive both actual UI resizes. Other config edits, CPU and ordinary
CUDA retain their previous restart policy. Settled/final physical defaults were
not changed; this is temporary interaction resolution, not a final-quality cut.

Stock Blender5.3beta `5d8151335b6b`, actual RTX5060Ti CUDA/NGX, original GenmaB
geometry/material/light datablocks, RGB viewport, depth25 and VC4/pool4:

| Current v4 action | First NN preview | Full-size NN after resize | First preview film |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| orbit1 |7.302s| — |582x434|
| orbit2..4 |4.228 /3.844 /4.217s| — |582x434|
| resize smaller |4.399s|15.577s|466x434 →932x869|
| resize restore |6.499s|20.475s|582x434 →1165x869|

All six actions retained the native session;12 SDK evaluations, actual `view_draw`,
controlled texture/readback errors.000306/.000676 (<.002), and full1165x869
restoration passed the original150s functional gate in127.982s. Max observed draw
callback 0.038734s (see exact receipt/result for authoritative value). The unchanged
baseline failed150s at the second resize (orbit13.71..14.14s); no speed ratio is
computed from that failure. v2/v3 passed but are superseded. v2's
`temporary_preview` field incorrectly compared resize output to the pre-resize
size; v3/v4 add target-size-aware metadata. Full-size resize acceptance criteria
were never relaxed.

This is Xvfb/llvmpipe software GL with real CUDA/NGX, not hardware-workstation
latency or real-time FPS proof. Live-region one-pixel projection/filtering still
fails its diagnostic oracle (v4 max.18767); controlled orthographic upload success
does not close that issue. Whole-window Xvfb screenshot is black and excluded.
Actual uploaded/full-resolution images were directly inspected via fixed stock
Blender5.2 ACES2; reopened32F EXRs have error0. Glass/railings retain strong
low-sample noise and the floor is softened, so beauty quality remains false.

### CPU/Metal and regression boundary

23 unit/regression cases passed. The existing resize fixture needed native
`GetBool` and an explicit ordinary-DLSS helper stub; removing those two additions
reconstructs the exact prior WIP file. Actual current worker source on Mac passed
CPU and Metal64x48→96x48→80x48→64x48: CPU kept restart behavior, Metal kept the same
session, outputs were finite/nonblack and all workers drained. This is native
lifecycle evidence, not Blender-Metal UI, NVIDIA inference, image parity,4K or
standalone package proof. Four Mac supervised children exited0 and were reaped.

### HDR/component diagnosis — still failing quality

A separate actual GenmaB720p Camera.003 diagnostic produced exactly128 consecutive
lossless inputs/NGX evaluations, first reset only, with EMISSION, DIRECT_DIFFUSE,
DIRECT_GLOSSY, INDIRECT_DIFFUSE, INDIRECT_GLOSSY and INDIRECT_SPECULAR. It uses the
same explicit RGB eye/depth25 cache/LT/VC/MNEE/guiding/spectral-off diagnostic
transport as the previous finite references, which differs from the heavy UI's
VC-enabled workload. Do not compare their per-frame speeds.

The six physical AOVs sum to current raw RGB within5.9605e-6 (MSE1.6441e-15).
Against the previous two finite512SPP references, raw mean luminance differs
-0.005925%, RR -2.587605%; pixel MSE raw.00689434, RR.00264549, but16px-block MSE
raw.0000272893, RR.000408387. Visible-emission pixels account for7.264% of the net
signed luminance difference; indirect-diffuse-dominant regions34.505% and
indirect-specular-dominant regions45.764%. These conditional masks are not causal
attributions and have stochastic selection effects. The source-conditioned next
investigation is indirect/reflection/refraction guide/HDR behavior; correcting
visible emitters alone cannot explain the measured global loss.

Extra-AOV raw is NOT bit-exact to the earlier seed42 raw: max2.00236,
MSE.000441501. Export/sampling/compiler differences must be isolated; this is not
a strict preservation pass or proof that enabling AOVs is harmless. The two512
references share512 frame-ID camera jitters and retain noise/shared-error limits;
they are not ground truth. Diffuse `a/(1+a)` mapping matches pinned Cycles
`5c91e04354b2` kernel lines1221..1224, so that mapping alone is not a discovered
bug. No fitted gain/clamp, default promotion, or equal-error speed claim was added.
The new raw/RR/reference comparison and actual AOV images were directly reviewed.

### Preservation and resources

54 unrelated snapshot files are byte-identical; the sole additional existing test
change is the two-line fixture repair above. All181 CL kernels, native/runtime,
original blend SHA `f63059830408` and user-default addon513 ZIP members are
preserved. Only two production Python modules changed. No commit/push/release.

Six server children and owned displays/read/OIDN/session workers were reaped;
all UI/component temporary directories and quit.blend files were removed. About
898.8MB of obsolete remote image copies were removed only after matching exact
retained local SHA aliases. Obsolete own profile v1/v2/v3 installed copies were
removed, small recoverable archives retained, currentv4 kept; original wheels and
baseline7/10/12/13/14 remain. An obsolete phase4 installed SO has an exact retained
original-wheel member and an archived marker; unique historical d3a8 native remains.
Supervisors retain24GiB RAM,3GiB SSD and existing time limits. Unrelated resident
render work began after these tests and changed shared disk headroom; final audit
may show below3GiB. Do not launch another native test without fresh resource
admission, lower the guard, kill other jobs or derive timing ratios from contention.

Full scene/material/motion/final-animation/SR/4K/packaging gates, current physical
quality, workstation responsiveness, original Save/text/strict export issues and
full Metal157s cold-start proof remain open. DLSS5 NR remains a separate SDK/API
feature and is not a blocker or a support claim for this RR candidate.


## Phase 16 — final fallback ownership, paired emission diagnostics, and DLSS 5 scope (2026-10-17)

Evidence: workspace `dev-tools/reviews/2026-10-16/dlss/phase16/phase16-report.json`,
`source-identity.json`, `emission-analysis.json`, `preservation.json`, and
`checkpoint-in-progress.json`. Full goal remains **active**, physical image quality
is **not accepted**, and no speed ratio, commit, push, or release is claimed.

### Bounded production fix and actual Blender checks

`engine/dlss_final.py` SHA `25b8d1179f53` releases `result`, canonical alpha/raw RGB
scratch, and the previous consumer packet immediately on failed presentation,
invalidation, or close. Teardown uses `finally` so a backend-close exception still
releases Python arrays; invalidation's outer `finally` also releases lossless frame
delivery. Previously these arrays survived fallback until frame end, and a teardown
exception could leave delivery parked. No radiance estimator, sample settings,
image processing strength, or default selection changed.

Four deterministic ownership/failure regressions fail on the retained b18 helper
and pass on25b. Real NumPy3840x2160 allocations exercise132,710,400 bytes of alpha
and raw RGB scratch; all weak references disappear after cleanup. This **is not**
a4K Blender/NGX quality or device-loss pass. Peak supervised RSS983,908,352 bytes;
4GiB bound and60s deadline retained. Actual driver teardown faults remain untested.

Separate private profiles verified the actual25b helper against unchanged native
Linux `6a4c40302e7b`, Mac `e7bfc0d40fa1`, and optional runtime `58467c2dd1bb`:

- Stock Blender5.3beta/RTX5060Ti,1280x720 transparent F12,8 real NGX evaluations:
  normal output and canonical RGB/alpha/all10 raw AOVs match the paired control;
  partial coverage retains the existing Phase14 raw-RGB hybrid. EXR reopen passed.
- Actual320x180 CUDA F12, real NGX and native presentation on both first inputs;
  injected failure after the second native presentation releases921,600 scratch
  bytes immediately. Weak references are gone, the producer continues to the
  exact8-SPP target, all10 canonical AOVs remain bit-exact to control, and OIDN
  fallback completes. This validates a real native/SDK path with a deterministic
  presentation fault, not an actual GPU device-loss event.
- Stock Blender5.2 CPU and Metal320x180/8-SPP unsupported-DLSS cases complete via
  OIDN with0 SDK calls and no DLSS controller. This is fallback/lifecycle evidence,
  not Metal neural reconstruction or CPU/Metal performance parity.

Previous animation/complex-material/SR/alpha-background limits remain open; older
b18 animation results are not relabelled as fresh25b animation validation.

### Emission hypothesis tested, not promoted

Pinned official Cycles `5c91e04354b2` adds emissive contributions to denoising
features before surface-feature evaluation. SuperLuxCore's inspected guide path
lacks that addition, but source similarity alone does not establish a quality fix.
Two128-input actual GenmaB Camera.003720p experiments preserve each native packet
and evaluate independent NGX histories; identical B/B histories are bit-exact for
all256 observed frames. First reset only in each history. Total896 evaluations in
these experiments, plus10 from the fresh F12 checks above:906 actual evaluations.

The first experiment's source-conditioned black-closure primary mask covers
**zero** pixels in this scene. Its restricted delta-tail inference is diagnostic,
not a renderer-owned emission guide, and cannot validate primary emission.
The second obtains actual independent native EMISSION via the existing native
image-pipeline snapshot and OUTPUT_SWITCHER channel, without prefix subtraction
or deriving primary emission from beauty. Its128-frame mean matches canonical
accumulated EMISSION within7.6294e-6. Four histories compare baseline, identical
control, exact primary emission, and primary plus the restricted tail hypothesis.

Against the finite2x512 reference pair (512 shared frame-ID jitters, still noisy,
not ground truth), the second experiment yields:

|128-input result|Mean luminance error|Pixel MSE|16px block MSE|
|---|---:|---:|---:|
|Canonical raw|−0.01665%|0.00687421|0.0000276143|
|RR baseline|−2.54785%|0.00266013|0.000406493|
|Exact primary-emission feature|−2.58475%|0.00262874|0.000431893|
|Primary plus restricted tail|−2.58489%|0.00263271|0.000428695|

The primary-emission candidate slightly reduces pixel MSE but worsens broad-scale
error and signed brightness. **Neither hypothesis was promoted or added to the
production guide path.** Cross-run raw/export/sampling/compiler differences remain
open; within-packet B/B preservation does not close that separate gate. No fitted
gain, exposure matching, clamp, or default change was used.

All new raw/RR/feature images were exported and reopened as float EXR with zero
error, and viewed under the same stock ACES2/exposure0/gamma1 pipeline. Direct
inspection of `emission-visuals/genmab-emission-comparison.png` confirms noise
reduction but no compelling improvement in the shaded walls/floor/fine framing.
The next guide/HDR investigation includes checking official camera-normal space
semantics before a same-packet axis counterfactual; a handedness suspicion is
**not a confirmed bug**.

### DLSS 5 Neural Rendering is a separate, currently unimplemented feature

Current runtime creates `NVSDK_NGX_Feature_RayReconstruction` via CUDA.
NVIDIA's official DLSS5 announcement describes NR as optional and independent of
RR/SR/FG, with generated lighting/material detail, structure/tone controls and
masks. RTX5060Ti belongs to the announced RTX50 family; this does not establish
current Linux driver/API/runtime eligibility. Existing RR execution proves no NR
support. No NR-specific authorized SDK/runtime has been obtained or evaluated.

The official `nvpro-samples/vk_gltf_renderer` source pinned at
`4e02cfe5a759adb5d274e1ed88486aab5829e8df` gates NR on the separate beta header
`nvsdk_ngx_helpers_dlssnr_vk.h` (CMake342..351), probes
`NVSDK_NGX_Feature_DLSSNR`, and evaluates a separate Vulkan feature after RR on an
LDR display image. It accounts for depth/motion scale and optional control mask.
Streamline `625db07d4bca` declares `kFeatureDLSS_NR=1004`; that identifier alone is
not a usable NR backend. CUDA-NR interoperability is unestablished by these
inspected sources. Record: `dlss5-feasibility.json` with pinned source URLs.

Any future integration needs the official feature-specific package, real capability
probe and color/resource contract first. It should be a separately enabled creative
output stage with original physical radiance/AOVs preserved, rather than an implied
physical-accuracy fix or automatic replacement for the remaining RR work.
Official references: [NVIDIA DLSS5](https://www.nvidia.com/en-us/geforce/news/dlss-5-3d-guided-neural-rendering/),
[official sample](https://github.com/nvpro-samples/vk_gltf_renderer/blob/4e02cfe5a759adb5d274e1ed88486aab5829e8df/src/dlss.cpp#L459-L649).

## Phase 17 — correct NGX jitter compensation; quality and latest heavy gate remain open (2026-10-17)

Previous turn was progress (Phase16 ownership fix and actual Blender gates).

complete-support, commit, push or release claim is made.

### Confirmed sampling-coordinate mismatch and bounded fix

Pinned official Cycles `5c91e04354b294600bbf4321f95d4ac674441108`
`kernel/camera/camera.h:468` traces `raster += -pixel_jitter`, then its CUDA DLSS
backend passes `pixel_jitter` unchanged to NGX (`denoiser_dlss.cpp:432..433`).
SuperLuxCore traces `pixel + .5 + pixelJitter` (`pathoclbase_funcs.cl:316..317`,
matching CPU pathtracer), while the previous adapter forwarded the same positive
ray displacement as NGX's camera compensation. This sign mismatch is independent
of a finite-reference image metric.

Current `engine/dlss.py` SHA `11a32193dc012314fea94e6f3767c67f7a3847b2cb2d7ad4ba76d6f31c44db87`
negates only the two renderer ray offsets at the NGX boundary. It validates their
shape and explicitly documents low-level `DLSSFrame.jitter` as compensation.
Canonical rays, raw RGB/alpha/AOVs, camera jitter sequence, guides, exposures,
transport, native binaries, kernels and defaults are unchanged. Final helper25b,
worker269 and viewporta110 remain the accepted earlier implementations.

New compensation regression fails on the saved old7e adapter (first case0.5px
error) and passes after the fix.29 adapter/history/projection/stream/interaction/
cleanup tests pass. Actual CPU POSITION AOVs at three offsets confirm the positive
ray-displacement contract, with maximum projection error1.93e-5px. Actual Metal
RT packets across19 camera/material/resize observations satisfy the compensation
invariant within2.30e-5px and retain leases/raw accumulation/halt-stop behavior.
This does **not** close the original GenmaB Metal position/Save/text/export issue.

The proposed camera-normal Z flip was rejected: Cycles' Blender camera matrix
uses `tfm*scale(1,1,-1)`, matching LuxCore's right/up/forward LookAt basis for the
ordinary orthonormal camera. Cycles' CUDA guiding preprocess also passes weighted
normal components with sample scale rather than unit-normalizing them. Neither
an arbitrary normal-axis flip nor a confidence-erasing normalization was added.
Pinned source copies and hashes are in `cycles-source.json`.

### Capture/replay semantics corrected without rewriting historical evidence

New RT and tiled capture manifests declare `jitter_semantics=ngx-compensation`;
where the tiled writer stores both values, `ray_jitter` remains the actual positive
ray displacement and `jitter` is the NGX compensation. The GPU primary-guide test
also constructs the SDK frame with the compensating sign. No raw plane is altered.

Replay rejects unknown/untagged semantics **before** allocating a GPU backend.
Known old captures require an explicit `--legacy-jitter-space ray-displacement`
or `--legacy-jitter-space ngx-compensation` declaration. Do not guess from a file
name or relabel historical old-sign NN output as corrected. The existing host
surface-capture tool was preserved with the other unrelated WIP; its legacy output
needs this explicit declaration.3 focused replay tests pass;21 fresh actual CPU/
Metal capture entries decode with the declared compensation. Actual Metal primary
surface fixtures and the updated CPU two-frame capture writer pass their native
raw/guide checks. Total32 focused unit tests; these are not general NN quality gates.

## Phase20 — current GenmaB same-packet RR models

A private source-identical CUDA wrapper adds only immutable per-instance official
RR preset hints and an optional unit-exposure diagnostic. It does not modify
renderer rays, radiance, guides, jitter, shaders, physical settings or installed
backend. Original GenmaB geometry/materials/lights/Camera.003 are exported with
the **same explicit RGB eye-only diagnostic overrides** as prior finite references;
this is not an original-all-features render or heavy viewport performance test.
Current native6a, adapter11a/helper25b, final5ce4908 and stock5.3beta5d8151335b6b.
Six independent SDK histories consume the same128 immutable1SPP packets: default
A/B, default+unit exposure, official D/E/F. **All768 evaluations completed**;
A/B, unit exposure and F are bit-exact on all128. Only first input resets per
history, alpha and every input-plane SHA remain exact. Hints D/E are honored
with visibly/numerically different outputs. Default=F is an observation for this
runtime/input set, not a guarantee about future OTA/default behavior.

postprocessing finishes while a benign film reader owns session_lock, the old
nonblocking publish branch drops its valid output. Without another input packet
there is no result to display. The new regression fails before repair. Historical
Phase18 had a completed first frame absent from uploads, but that log alone does
not prove the exact dropping race; do not claim a measured historical speedup.
